| H11.1-1 | Enforce the single full run with the guard `scripts/full-run.mjs`, which `make check` starts before any step: it refuses while a task row of this checklist is in progress and lists each active ID with its task, refuses while tracked changes are uncommitted, refuses a second full run of the same tree (`git rev-parse HEAD^{tree}`) and names the earlier run, runs each target of `CHECK_TARGETS` with `make <target>` to its end and writes `var/full-run.json` (tree, commit, result, each target with its status and times) before and after each target, so a stopped run stays `incomplete`; `make rerun-failed` reruns only the targets of the current tree that did not pass and completes the result when they pass. Cause: AGENTS states that `make check` runs exactly once, when every active task is done, and nothing enforced it: a run with a task in progress, a run of uncommitted changes and a second run of the same tree all started the suite. Red: `make -n check` printed the first step of `template-check`, so the suite started whatever the checklist held, `make -n rerun-failed` failed with `No rule to make target`, and `tests/scripts/full-run.test.mjs` failed with `ERR_MODULE_NOT_FOUND` for `scripts/full-run.mjs`. Green: its 10 cases pass: `make -n check` prints only the guard, a fixture checklist with a task in progress, a dirty tree, a second run of one tree and `rerun-failed` without a record are refused before any stub target runs, and `rerun-failed` runs only the stub targets that failed or did not finish | — | `node scripts/run-tests.mjs node -- tests/scripts/full-run.test.mjs` | [o] |

| H13.1 | Refuse a push while a task is in progress: the tracked pre-push hook `.githooks/pre-push` runs `node scripts/push-gate.mjs hook`, which parses the checklist of every pushed commit and of the working tree with `activeItems` of `scripts/full-run.mjs` and refuses the push with each active ID and its task, also when a pushed commit has no checklist; every `make` run sets `core.hooksPath` to `.githooks`, and `make hooks-check`, which `make docs-check` runs, and the guard of the full run fail while it is not set; the job `push-gate` of `.github/workflows/push-gate.yml` runs `node scripts/push-gate.mjs commit <sha>` on every push and pull request and fails for a task in progress and for a hook that the commit does not track as executable | — | `node scripts/run-tests.mjs node -- tests/scripts/push-gate.test.mjs tests/scripts/full-run.test.mjs` | [o] |
| H13.2 | HY-94: run the document and checklist rules in the required check. The job `push-gate` ran only `make push-gate-commit`, so a commit whose checklist had a state marker outside a task state passed the check that the ruleset `main` requires. The job runs `make docs-check` after it, with `if: ${{ !cancelled() }}`; the document checks read no network. `push-gate.test.mjs` runs the make steps of the job on a commit with such a marker and requires a failure that names the checklist line, and requires every step to pass without it | — | `node scripts/run-tests.mjs node -- tests/scripts/push-gate.test.mjs` | [o] |
| H13.4 | HY-94, HY-91: publish every change through a pull request and the merge queue. The ruleset `main` of `.github/ruleset.json` requires a pull request (no approval, every merge method, because `gh pr merge --auto` asks for SQUASH), the merge queue with `REBASE`, a linear history and the checks `push-gate` and `check (<group>)` of every CI group (integration 15368), and refuses a force-push and a deletion without a bypass actor; the declaration sets `allow_rebase_merge`, `allow_auto_merge` and `delete_branch_on_merge`, which `make github-ruleset` applies and `make github-ruleset-check` compares. `ci.yml` runs on `pull_request` and `merge_group` and cancels only pull request runs; `push-gate.yml` runs on `push` without `gh-readonly-queue/**`, `pull_request` and `merge_group`. | — | `node scripts/run-tests.mjs node -- tests/scripts/github-ruleset.test.mjs tests/scripts/ci-workflow.test.mjs tests/scripts/push-gate.test.mjs` | [o] |
| H13.4-1 | HY-91, HY-94: declare the triggers of each workflow exactly. The `on:` block of `.github/workflows/ci.yml` is `pull_request`, `merge_group` and `workflow_dispatch`, so the full suite also runs on a manual run, and the `on:` block of `.github/workflows/push-gate.yml` is `push` with `branches-ignore: ['gh-readonly-queue/**']`, `pull_request` and `merge_group`; no other workflow exists. The ruleset `main` requires `push-gate` and the four `check (<group>)` jobs of `ci.yml`. Red: the new case of `ci-workflow.test.mjs` failed for `ci.yml` without `workflow_dispatch`. Green: the cases of `ci-workflow.test.mjs` and `push-gate.test.mjs` pass | — | `make test-scripts TESTS="tests/scripts/ci-workflow.test.mjs tests/scripts/push-gate.test.mjs"` and `make docs-check` | [o] |
| H13.5 | Release a tagged commit of `main`: every change reaches `main` through the merge queue with the required checks, and the maintainer releases by tagging a commit of `main` `vX.Y.Z` after a version-bump pull request. The changelogs keep `## Unreleased` above the released versions, the job `ci-passed` of `ci.yml` passes only when every other job passed and the ruleset `main` requires exactly `push-gate` and `ci-passed`, and `.github/workflows/release.yml` checks the tagged commit and creates the GitHub Release with the package archives. Sub-items H13.5-1, H13.5-2, H13.5-3, H13.5-4, H13.5-5, H13.5-6, H13.5-7 and H13.5-8 | — | `make test-scripts TESTS="tests/scripts/ci-run.test.mjs tests/scripts/ci-workflow.test.mjs tests/scripts/github-ruleset.test.mjs tests/scripts/release.test.mjs"` and `make docs-check` | [o] |
| H13.5-1 | Keep `## Unreleased` at the top of both changelogs: `CHANGELOG.md` holds every entry under `## Unreleased`, because no version of this repository is released, and `CHANGELOG.ko.md` named that section `## 미배포`. Both name it `## Unreleased`, the heading that the release pull request renames to `## X.Y.Z`, and every change adds its entry there | — | `make docs-check` | [o] |
| H13.5-2 | HY-94: require one check for `ci.yml`. The ruleset `main` required `push-gate` and the four `check (<group>)` jobs of `ci.yml`, so a job added to `ci.yml` was not required until the ruleset named it. The last job `ci-passed` needs every other job, runs under `if: ${{ always() }}` on `ubuntu-26.04-arm` and runs `make ci-passed RESULTS='${{ toJSON(needs) }}'`; `scripts/ci-run.mjs passed` fails unless every needed job has the result `success`, naming each other job and result. The ruleset requires exactly `push-gate` and `ci-passed`. Red: the new cases of `ci-run.test.mjs`, `ci-workflow.test.mjs` and `github-ruleset.test.mjs` failed without the target, the job and the declaration. Green: they pass | — | `make test-scripts TESTS="tests/scripts/ci-run.test.mjs tests/scripts/ci-workflow.test.mjs tests/scripts/github-ruleset.test.mjs"` and `make docs-check` | [o] |
| H13.5-3 | HY-95: release a tag through a workflow. No workflow ran on a tag, so a release had no check that its commit passed the required checks on `main`, that the manifests and the changelog carried its version, or that its archives were built from it. `.github/workflows/release.yml` runs on the push of a tag `v*` or `*/v*` with the permission `contents: write`, sets up the job `check` of `ci.yml` and runs `make release-verify` (the tagged commit is an ancestor of `origin/main`, and the latest check runs `push-gate` and `ci-passed` of the commit concluded `success`), `make release-versions` (X.Y.Z in `package.json`, `packages/hyper-js/package.json` and `packages/hyper-node/package.json`, `packages/hyper-php/composer.json` without `version` taking the tag, and `## X.Y.Z` in `CHANGELOG.md`), `make release-assets` (`make packages`, `npm pack` and a `git archive` zip, named `<package name>-<version>.<ext>`) and `make release-publish` (`gh release create --verify-tag` with the section as notes); `scripts/release.mjs` names each failed check and each file with both versions. A release step without `TAG` fails before any prerequisite. AGENTS states the release procedure. Red: the cases of `release.test.mjs` and the new case of `ci-workflow.test.mjs` failed without the script, the targets and the workflow. Green: they pass | — | `make test-scripts TESTS="tests/scripts/release.test.mjs tests/scripts/ci-workflow.test.mjs"` and `make docs-check` | [o] |
| H13.5-4 | HY-95: release a Go module tag at any depth and only npm and Composer archives. In a tag filter `*` does not match `/`, so the trigger `tags: ['v*', '*/v*']` matched a tag with one `/` only. The trigger of `.github/workflows/release.yml` is `tags: ['v*', '**/v*']`; the release assets are npm tarballs and Composer zips only, and a Cargo package is not released as an archive but consumed by git tag, because `cargo package` rewrites git dependencies into crates.io requirements that do not resolve; `scripts/release.mjs` lists a tracked `Cargo.toml` in `NOT_RELEASED` as not released as an archive; consumed by git tag. Red: the trigger case of `ci-workflow.test.mjs` failed with `*/v*`, and the asset case of `release.test.mjs` failed without `GIT_TAG`. Green: they pass | — | `make test-scripts TESTS="tests/scripts/release.test.mjs tests/scripts/ci-workflow.test.mjs"` and `make docs-check` | [o] |
| H13.5-5 | HY-95: release 0.0.1. The manifests `package.json`, `packages/hyper-js/package.json` and `packages/hyper-node/package.json` declare 0.0.1 and `packages/hyper-php/composer.json` has no `version` field; `## Unreleased` of `CHANGELOG.md` and `CHANGELOG.ko.md` becomes `## 0.0.1`, with a new empty `## Unreleased` above it. Evidence: `make ext` exit 0 and `php -d extension=build/ext/polyspec_template.so` loads `polyspec_template`; `make test-php` exit 0 (PHPUnit with the native extension: 318 passed, 1 skipped); `make analyse-php` exit 0 (No errors). | — | `make ext`, `make test-php`, `make analyse-php`, `make test-scripts TESTS="tests/scripts/template-ext.test.mjs tests/scripts/template-pins.test.mjs"` | [o] |
| H15.1 | HY-92, HY-31, HY-13, HY-54: a document embeds its data only when the reply of its request asks for it. `Reply::embedData()` (PHP) and `reply.embedData()` (Node) record the choice; `renderDocument` of the browser package takes `{ embed }`, the Node server passes the choice of the reply and client-side rendering passes `false`, because the browser holds the data of the JSON. The board example embeds the data of the post page and none on the list, `scripts/check-parity.mjs` renders the document with the choice of the server, and the comparison page of the board digests the body without `#hy-data`. Cause: every server-rendered document of a route with a present route region embedded its data, so the source of a page showed its data and the HTML was larger. Red: `AppTest` failed 6 cases with 500 (`Reply::embedData` did not exist) and `app.test.ts` and `client.test.ts` 6 cases; `response.test.ts` failed 2 cases and `hyper.test.ts` 1 case, because the document of `/list` embedded its data without the option. Green: 318 PHPUnit, 335 Node and 217 browser cases pass | — | `make test-php`, `make test-node`, `make test-js` | [o] |
| H15.2 | HY-93, HY-32, HY-33, HY-35, HY-36, HY-38: the browser obtains the data that it does not hold with one document request for the URL of the current page. `Hyper.open()` starts a page: client-side rendering renders its document, a server-rendered page holds its embedded data, and without it requests the data at load only when a `localStorage` or `sessionStorage` value is stored for a route region of its route. `data(region)` returns a promise; `data`, `render`, `set` and `hy-set` on a route region of the current page obtain the data first, calls during the request wait for it, the browser renders every present route region from the obtained data, a region response that replaced the held data meanwhile wins, and a failed request marks the region or the body and is sent again by the next change. The new module `packages/hyper-js/src/obtain.ts` sends the request. The entry templates no longer hold `hyper/data.tpl`, which the Node server checks itself; the board client calls `open()`, and the browser benchmark waits for `#rows`. Cause: after H15.1 a server-rendered page without embedded data held nothing, so `hy-set` and kept values failed. Red: 14 cases of `hyper.test.ts` failed: `open` did not exist, `set` failed with `no response data is held`, and the entry templates held `hyper/data.tpl`. Green: 229 browser cases and 335 Node cases pass | — | `make test-js` | [o] |

| H15.3-20 | HY-67: the Python server calls a disconnect hook. `App.open` of `polyspec.hyper.app` takes `on_disconnect`, which fails when it is not callable, and `App.respond` answers a request as `handle` does and returns the response with the reply of the request; `handle` returns its response. `create_server` sends the response that `respond` returned, and when a write of it fails with `BrokenPipeError`, `ConnectionResetError` or `ConnectionAbortedError` it calls `App.disconnected` with the request, the start of the request and the reply, which calls the hook once with the elapsed milliseconds. The response hook (HY-60) runs before the write, and a close is not detected when every write succeeds. `tests/render/render_server.py` serves the application with `create_server` on 127.0.0.1 and a port that the system assigns: a client closes the connection after the response hook started and before it reads a response of 6 MB, and another client reads the whole response. Red: the 3 cases of `render_server.py` failed with `TypeError: App.open() got an unexpected keyword argument 'on_disconnect'`. Green: the 3 cases pass, run with `PYTHONPATH=packages/hyper-python/src` and the template package of the tag `v0.0.5`. | — | `make test-python-render` | [o] |
| H15.3-21 | HY-97: the Python server takes a request hook around the answer of a request, as a consumer of the Node server wraps the `request` listener of the server that `app.server()` returns. `create_server` takes `around`, which fails when it is not callable; the server calls it with the request that it built and a function `answer(request)` that runs the application for a request with the session of the request and the cookie of a new session, and sends the `Response` that `around` returns. `Request.with_header(name, value)` returns a copy with the header set, the name compared without case. The disconnect hook (HY-67) receives the request and the reply of the last call of `answer`, and is not called when `around` answered alone. `tests/render/render_server.py` serves with `around` that answers alone, that changes the request and the response, that runs `answer` in a context of its own, and that answers alone to a client that closes the connection; `tests/test_modules.py` checks `Request.with_header`. Red: the 6 cases of `AroundTest` failed with `TypeError: create_server() got an unexpected keyword argument 'around'`, and the case of `with_header` failed with `AttributeError: 'Request' object has no attribute 'with_header'`. Green: the 9 cases of `render_server.py` and the 60 cases of `make test-python` pass, run with `PYTHONPATH=packages/hyper-python/src` and the template package of the tag `v0.0.5`. | — | `make test-python`, `make test-python-render` | [o] |

| H19.9 | The Node server writes a 500 without calling the response hook for an error that escapes the answer of a request: the `catch` of `serve` in `packages/hyper-server-node/src/http.ts` writes it, and a failed session open writes one too. The hook never receives that response with its request, so a consumer that records every answered request cannot record it. Cause: HY-60 names the 500 of HY-43 and HY-66 but no rule says which response the Node server writes for an error that escapes the answer; Python writes no response for the same error (HY-97, HY-99), and PHP lets the error end the request. Rule: HY-60 states that the Node server writes no response for such an error, logs it with the message of HY-43 and closes the connection, and calls no further hook for that request. Fix: `serve` and the session open of `packages/hyper-server-node/src/http.ts` log and close the connection instead of writing a 500. A handler failure already reaches the hook with its request and its 500, and the test keeps that path. | — | `node scripts/kit/run-tests.mjs vitest --cwd packages/hyper-server-node tests/http.test.ts`, `make dependency-policy-check`, `make documents-check`  Red: `node scripts/kit/run-tests.mjs vitest --cwd packages/hyper-server-node tests/http.test.ts` ended with `vitest: 11 passed, 1 failed`; the failing test `writes no response for an error that the response hook throws, logs it and calls no further hook (HY-60)` reported `promise resolved "Response { status: 500, statusText: 'Internal Server Error'`, where the rule requires no response. Green: the same command printed `vitest: 12 passed, 0 failed, 0 timed out, 0 skipped`; `node scripts/tsc.mjs --noEmit -p packages/hyper-server-node/tsconfig.json` exited 0; `PYTHONPATH=packages/hyper-server-python/src python3 packages/hyper-server-python/tests/run.py packages/hyper-server-python/tests test_escape.py` printed `Ran 1 test` and `OK`; `make dependency-policy-check` printed `12 registry dependencies and 4 locks match the review`; `make documents-check` printed `15 document pairs, 1 checklists, 0 status tables and 1 changelogs passed`. | [o] |
| H19.9-1 | The error that escapes the answer of a request is answered with a plain 500 by the Node server and the Python `create_server`, as the production engines answer it (gunicorn for a WSGI callable, php-fpm with nginx for PHP), not closed without a response. H19.9 closed the connection, and nginx answers a closed upstream connection with 502, which differs from the other engines. HY-60 states the rule: the server logs the error with the message of HY-43 and writes a plain 500 with no body detail when the response has not started, reports that 500 to no hook, and closes the connection when the response has started or the write of the 500 fails. The Node `serve` catch of `packages/hyper-server-node/src/http.ts` and the Python `serve` of `packages/hyper-server-python/src/polyspec/hyper/server.py` implement it, and the session open of Node has no separate path. | H19.9-2 | An error that escapes the answer of a request gets hyper's own plain 500 in every adapter: status 500, the text `Internal Server Error`, `Cache-Control: no-store` and the frame policy (HY-60). The WSGI and ASGI callables (HY-100, HY-101) write it before `start_response` or their first send instead of propagating the error, and the Python write hook (HY-99) receives it once with the request as received, also when `around` never ran. The Node server writes it through its `node:http` response, so its write events receive it and it needs no code change. PHP `App::run` writes it for an uncaught exception (`set_exception_handler`) and for a fatal error of the request (a shutdown function reads `error_get_last()`), when headers have not been sent, and reports it to the response hook only when that hook has not run for the request. Cause: the Python callables let the error propagate to their server, `create_server` gave the write hook no report for an escaped error, and PHP let such an error reach the web server's 500. Depends on H19.9-1. | — | `make test-python PYTHON=<absolute path of var/tools/bin/python3>`, `make test-node`, `make test-php`, `make test-python-render PYTHON=<absolute path of var/tools/bin/python3>`, `make analyse-php`, `make package-check`, `make dependency-policy-check`, `make documents-check`  Red: `make test-python` ran 87 unit tests and failed with `failures=2, errors=8`; `test_a_failed_session_open_is_reported_before_the_application_runs` and `test_an_error_that_escapes_the_answer_gets_the_plain_500_and_one_write_report` failed on the missing write report, and the WSGI and ASGI tests of a failed body read, a missing target, a failing `around` and a failed send raised the error instead of answering a 500. `make test-php` ran 321 tests and failed with `Failures: 3`: `testAFatalErrorBeforeTheResponseHookWritesThePlain500AndReportsIt`, `testAFatalErrorAfterTheResponseHookWritesThePlain500WithoutASecondReport` and `testAnUncaughtExceptionAfterTheResponseHookWritesThePlain500WithoutASecondReport`. The Node test `reports the plain 500 of an escaped error to the write events of its response with its request (HY-60, HY-99)` passed at its first run, which shows that the Node server already gives the write events of that 500. Green: `make test-python` printed `Ran 87 tests` with `OK` and `Ran 2 tests` with `OK`; `make test-node` printed `vitest: 338 passed, 0 failed, 0 timed out, 0 skipped` and its type check passed; `make test-php` printed `321 passed, 0 failed, 0 timed out, 0 skipped` for the generated program and `321 passed, 0 failed, 0 timed out, 1 skipped` for the native extension, where `RendererTest::testLoadsTheGeneratedProgramFromItsNamespace` skipped before this change too; `make test-python-render` printed `Ran 32 tests` with `OK`; `make analyse-php` printed `[OK] No errors`; `make package-check` printed `node --test: 4 passed, 0 failed, 0 timed out, 0 skipped`; `make dependency-policy-check` printed `12 registry dependencies and 4 locks match the review of 2026-10-10T17:40:42.499Z; 0 stable-version exceptions; no advisory`; `make documents-check` printed `15 document pairs, 1 checklists, 0 status tables and 1 changelogs passed`. | [o] |
| H19.9-3 | HY-99 states how a PHP consumer receives the write result of a response: PHP has no write hook, because php-fpm sends the response and hyper emits no write event, so a consumer registers its own shutdown function, which knows its request from the start and reads `http_response_code()` there, as HY-67 does for the disconnect hook. The rule is in `docs/spec/protocol.md` and `docs/spec/protocol.ko.md`; no code changes. | H20.1 | Rules of the write hook and the plain 500: HY-60 keeps the headers that the caller set before hyper received the request (H1), HY-99 gives every server the write hook `written` with its values and its order before the disconnect hook, HY-100 and HY-101 pass the escaped 500 of their callables to `start_response` and `send`, and HY-102 names `App.listener` of the Node server; the rules are written in English and Korean `make documents-check`  Red: before the rules, `docs/spec/protocol.md` had no HY-102 (`grep -c HY-102` printed 0), and with a stale Korean marker `make documents-check` printed `1 findings in 15 document pairs` and exited 2. | — | Green: `make documents-check` printed `15 document pairs, 1 checklists, 0 status tables and 1 changelogs passed`. | [o] |

The first chunk of the board showed that `scripts/serve-edge.mts` served a `.js` file as `application/octet-stream`, which the browser refuses as a module script, so the CSR flow of `make e2e` failed; the edge serves it as `text/javascript; charset=utf-8`, immutable like a template file, as `docs/operations/deployment.md` states, and the data flow of `make e2e` counts a chunk file as a static asset that the page loads at most once (HY-76). | H28.11-10 | Specify and implement how the three servers read a request field that holds one value and arrives in several field lines, the same in every server, so that no line is kept or dropped without a failure (P8); only `Accept` (HY-116.4) and `Cookie` (HY-129.3 to HY-129.6) state their lines today. Cause: a request with two `Content-Type` lines gives the body type of its first line in the Node server and in the Python `create_server`, of its last line under the WSGI callable on gunicorn, and the two values joined under PHP and the ASGI callable, and no server fails it. The Node server reads `incoming.headers` of `node:http` (`http-host.ts:110`, `:113`, `:115`, `:272`), which keeps the first line of `content-type`, `content-length`, `authorization`, `host`, `user-agent`, `referer` and the twelve other names that `node:http` reads as one value, discards their other lines, and joins the lines of every other name with `, `; the WSGI callable reads `CONTENT_TYPE` and the `HTTP_` keys of the environ (`wsgi.py:147`, `:158`, `:206-215`), where gunicorn 26.2.0 writes the last `Content-Type` line and joins the lines of another name with `,`; the Python `create_server` reads `Content-Type` and `Content-Length` with `get` of the message, which gives the first line (`server.py:103`, `:115`, `:117`), and joins the lines of every name with `, ` (`server.py:243`); the ASGI callable joins with `, ` (`asgi.py:246-254`); PHP reads the `HTTP_` keys of `$_SERVER` (`Request.php:505-517`), which nginx 1.23.0 and later writes with the lines of one name joined by `, `. (4) The servers give the answers of HY-113.23, HY-113.24, HY-113.25, HY-131.28 and HY-131.30, the cases that `docs/plans/execution/plan.md` lists enter `contracts/request.json`, and the rows `form-failures` and `form-kinds` of `docs/features.md` become implemented. The bytes of a body are read under the limits before the route, for every request, and the `Content-Type` is judged after the route, for an action and `/_hyper/keep` by HY-59.12 and for a representation by HY-121.11; the reader of http-form for a body in chunks starts before the route only for `multipart/form-data` with a boundary, a `Content-Type` that is no media type fails no reading, and an unknown path answers 404 whatever its `Content-Type` (HY-57.15). (7) `malformed_content_type` stays the kind of hyper of HY-59.12 and HY-59.13 in the table of HY-132.3, because hyper gives no such `Content-Type` to a function of http-form that fails with that kind. The build keeps `client/assets.json`, its own list, and `client/assets.previous.json`, the list of the last build before it whose list differed. A build whose list differs removes, after it published, exactly the files that `assets.previous.json` named and that neither the list of the build before nor its own list names, and prints each. A build of unchanged sources moves no list and removes nothing. A build with fewer than two earlier lists removes nothing, and no file is removed for the form of its name. Before it writes a file, a build whose list differs records every path of the three lists in `assets.previous.json`, so a build that ends early leaves no file without a list. An earlier list of another form fails the build before it writes, with the file, the path and the repair. The build id leaves out `client/assets.previous.json`. | H35.75 | `make pin VERSIONS="<package>=<version> ..."` takes the versions of the sibling polyspec releases that config/pins.json declares: binding, json, template, session-store, php-serialization, php-serialize, http-cookie, http-negotiation and http-media-type; a package that it does not name keeps its pin, and a package that config/pins.json does not declare, such as orm, which hyper names nowhere, is refused. The input is one variable because BINDING already names the binding generator in the Makefile. It writes config/pins.json and every place that names one of those versions: the release URLs of every package.json and the plain versions of packages/*/package.json, the git tags of packages/rust/Cargo.toml, the Composer repository URLs and the config.platform versions of the extensions, TEMPLATE_VERSION and JSON_VERSION of the Makefile, the version fields of config/php-ext-pins.json, and the wheel URLs of pyproject.toml with the sha256 of each from the field digest of its release asset. It writes no lock and no file of a binding itself: it runs make npm-lock, install-packages, cargo-lock, bindings-scaffold, composer-lock, php-ext-pins and python-lock in that order, as the change needs. A missing digest or an undeclared package stops scripts/pin.mts before it writes. When a lock target refuses, make pin stops, names the target and its lines, and keeps what it wrote; the rewrite is idempotent, so a second run with the same versions finishes the same work, and a run with the declared versions writes nothing. `make pin` downloads, so it is an installation target of HR-89 whose downloads go through $(ONLINE), as the lock targets do, and the list of installation targets of AGENTS.md and AGENTS.ko.md gains it in the same change. Red first: a scripts test with a fixture tree that holds one place of each kind, which states the rewrite of each kind, the run that writes nothing and the refusal of a wheel without its digest. | scripts/pin.mts, Makefile, tests/scripts/pin.test.mts, tests/scripts/fixtures/pin, docs/operations/dependencies.md, AGENTS.md, AGENTS.ko.md, CHANGELOG.md | `make hyper-test-scripts TESTS=tests/scripts/pin.test.mts` | [ ] |

Every lock goal that scripts/pin.mts names is run: one cargo-lock names all changed crates, and the goals fit the two make calls of make pin. | scripts/pin.mts, tests/scripts/pin.test.mts, tests/scripts/fixtures/pin | `make hyper-test-scripts TESTS=tests/scripts/pin.test.mts` | [v] |

| H35.87 | No server removes an expired session while it answers a request, and the application removes them by calling `collect` of its store (HY-104.33, SF-5). The PHP server sets `gc_probability` to 0 in the options of `session_start`, the Python store names the method `collect` instead of `_collect`, the Node store already has a public `collect`, and the entry `file-sessions.collect` of `contracts/interfaces.json` is public. The PHP server has the public static `NativeSession::collect`, which calls `collect` of `Polyspec\SessionStore\FileSessions`, made by `FileSessions::inDirectory` with `session.save_path`, or the temporary directory when it is empty as for the native files handler, and `session.gc_maxlifetime`; it returns the count that session-store removed and fails with `SessionStoreIoError` for a directory that cannot be read (SF-9.2). It reads the save path as the native files handler does: `0;/path` and `0;MODE;/path` collect `/path`, and a depth above 0 fails with `invalid_directory` (SF-1.2). Under a `session.save_handler` other than `files` it fails with a `HyperValueError` of kind `invalid_session_handler` that names the handler (HY-104.44, HY-132.14). The PHP run waits for the pin of session-store 0.0.8, which holds that class for PHP. Cause: the PHP options left `gc_probability` at its default, so the native handler removed expired files during a request by chance, and the Python method was private, so an application could not call it; with `gc_probability` 0 a PHP application had no way to remove expired sessions. | packages/php/src/Session/NativeSession.php, packages/php/tests/RequestTest.php, packages/php/tests/NativeSessionTest.php, packages/python/src/polyspec/hyper/session/file_sessions.py, packages/python/tests/steps/file_sessions_steps.py, tests/scripts/session-shared-redis.test.mts, tests/scripts/fixtures/session-worker/worker.php, contracts/protocol.json, docs/spec/protocol.md and its Korean twin, contracts/interfaces.json, CHANGELOG.md and its Korean twin | `make hyper-test-php`, `make hyper-test-python`, `make hyper-test-scripts TESTS=tests/scripts/session-shared-redis.test.mts` | [ ] |
